just wanted to share and experience with you.
dpd post doesn't send me my deliveries (they send bullshit messages like we couldn't find you in your apartment although i was at home) and didn't even answered my complaints, made me pissed off on them!  
Since it was the second time having serious issue with dpd posting service, I decided to deal with them proprerly so I kept annoying the seller and Amazon to make this irresponsible post work better in the future and face the consequnces when they ignore our messages!

This is what amazon told me:
"In any case, I have noted this feedback with the order above.

The selection of the carriers, when you make the order, is automatic, based on your address and country.
If you repeatedly experience same issues with particular carrier, you can send us 3 order number in the last 6 months where the carrier was the same, so we can forward this to dispatch team.

They can check if they can remove this carrier from your future orders, so you avoid same issues again."
So if you have also had the same issure with dpd you can report it to the amazon customer service via impressum@amazon.de and they will process your inquiry.

Btw Folks,
Germany follows a strict waste separation system to conserve resources and optimize recycling. The main waste categories are:

Yellow bin (Gelber Sack): Plastic packaging, metal packaging (e.g., cans, aluminum), composite materials (e.g., Tetra Paks). No general waste, no food waste, no paper.

Blue bin: Paper and cardboard (newspapers, cartons, office paper). No coated paper (e.g., greasy pizza boxes, baking paper).

Organic bin (Bio bin) brown top : Organic waste like food scraps (except meat, fish, and bones), fruit and vegetable peels, coffee grounds, garden waste. *No plastic bags!!!!*(unless compostable).

Residual waste bin: Everything that cannot be recycled, e.g., hygiene products, vacuum cleaner bags, cigarette butts, broken ceramics.

Glass containers:

White glass: Clear glass bottles and jars (no ceramics, porcelain, or window glass).

Green glass: Green glass bottles (also for blue bottles).

Brown glass: Brown glass

[12/28/25, 8:58:52 PM] +49 1512 0721592: Hot water email for howoge 

(Note: 19.12 is when I reported it, idk if this legal stuff applies to you when you didn't inform them, I know other people called too though, maybe change it to the date when you called/reported it on their website) 

[your name, address etc]

Mängelanzeige, Mietminderung

Sehr geehrtes Team der Howoge,

[my description maybe change that to your own: ] seit dem 19.12.2025 funktioniert das warme Wasser im gesamten Gebäude in der Eichbuschallee 55 nicht, der Schaden wurde bereits mehrmals von mehreren Nachbarn telefonisch und über das Online Portal gemeldet. Es wurde mehrmals versichert, der Schaden würde schnell behoben werden, ist aber unverändert.

[lawyer text copy paste from last year] Da Sie gemäß § 535 BGB verpflichtet sind, Ihre Häuser und Wohnungen in einem mangelfreien Zustand zu halten, fordere ich Sie auf, die o.g. bezeichneten Mängel bis spätestens zum 31.12.2025 beseitigen zu lassen. Die Berechnung der Frist von 3 Werktagen, ergibt sich aus der Dringlichkeit aufgrund der aktuellen Wetterlage und da Ihnen der Ausfall des Warmwassers seit dem 19.12.2025 bekannt ist.

Wegen der erheblichen Einschränkungen des Wohnwertes meiner Wohnung durch die o.g.  Mängel steht mir gemäß § 536 BGB ein Mietminderungsrecht zu.

Da die weitere Beeinträchtigung des Wohnwertes für mich nicht vorhersehbar ist, werde ich die Mietzahlungen ab sofort unter dem Vorbehalt der Rückforderung leisten und nach Beendigung der Reparaturmaßnahmen den Mietminderungsanspruch insgesamt rückwirkend geltend machen.

Mit freundlichen Grüßen,


Send to:
kundenzentrum@howoge.de
studenthouse@howoge.de

[12/29/25, 5:17:55 PM] +49 176 20491747: In Germany, racist actions — especially when they go beyond mere rude language and target you because of your race, ethnicity, religion or similar characteristics — can be criminal offences and the person can face penalties under criminal law. The exact penalty depends on what exactly happened, but here are the main possibilities:

🔹 1. Hate-motivated insults / Racist “hate speech” (§ 130 StGB)

If someone publicly incites hatred, insults or degrades you because of your race, ethnicity, nationality, religion, sexual orientation, etc., this can fall under the offence of incitement to hatred (“Volksverhetzung”).
	•	This is a criminal offence.
	•	It can be punished by up to 5 years in prison or a fine.  ￼

🔹 2. Insult (§ 185 StGB)

Even without the hate-speech rules, simply insulting someone in Germany can be a punishable offence:
	•	Beleidigung (insult) can lead to a fine or imprisonment.
	•	For insults committed publicly or via media (e.g., social networks), the maximum can be up to 2 years in prison or a fine.  ￼

🔹 3. Other related offences

Depending on behaviour, other crimes may apply:
	•	Threats (§ 241 StGB) — threatening violence can be punished.  ￼
	•	Coercion / Nötigung (§ 240 StGB) — if someone uses threats or force.  ￼
	•	Defamation or slander (§ 186/187 StGB) — if false harmful statements are made about you.  ￼

⚖️ Criminal vs. Civil Action
	•	A criminal process can be started by reporting the incident to the police; authorities may investigate and the public prosecutor can pursue the case.
	•	You can also pursue civil remedies (e.g., claim damages) separately with a lawyer.

📌 Important Notes
	•	Minor insults — unfortunately — are often not prosecuted unless they clearly meet the legal thresholds.  ￼
	•	Penalties vary a lot based on the severity, circumstances, and evidence.
	•	Hate-speech provisions apply especially when the abuse is targeted at protected characteristics (race, religion, etc.) — these carry heavier penalties.  ￼

⸻

If you want, you can describe what exactly the person said/did, and I can help you understand which specific offence(s) it might be and what that means in practice.

[1/9/26, 2:54:27 PM] +49 1525 3599175: You should also not be afraid of losing your apartments. This is a matter of tenancy law. The landlord wants his rent, you want a functional apartment. If this relationship is disrupted, you just have to move on and see what exactly is wrong. 
In Germany, there is very strong tenant protection. But this can be very stressful, time-consuming, and, above all, tedious. That is why it is sometimes very difficult to achieve success without legal assistance. 
However, there is the Tenant Protection Association in Berlin, which also offers tenant protection insurance.
https://www.mieterschutzbund-berlin.de/

[3/18/26, 2:26:51 AM] +90 531 279 57 01: Mine does that too it started to happen around when we had an outage in all the blocks and then I realized it would stay blue no matter what however it actually reset, weird thing is when unplugged and plugged back in it would go through orange green blue cycle and all. Ever since Ive been having LAN issues today I was kinda fed up with it and tried things and these might not be universal but I kid you not we’re getting tangoed… Here’s what’s up at least for me, I’ve tried using my own router instead of the issued wall mount and it worked both through the wall embedded ethernet and directly where it did not prior to that the second and vital thing is I saw that I got around 30mbps with it which is the same amount Ive been getting for the last couple weeks with the devices that it worked somehow whereas even the wifi which I dont even get from my own router for some reason probably RADIUS or some shit is faster than that (40ish) now I wouldnt complain just for that by itself but it is hella weird that its faster on wifi and even weirder when you take into consideration that I had around 800mbps before all these. To my surprise when I got rid of my router as well and hooked it up directly to my pc without any interface in between I saw it jump to around 350. All in all these tell me two things, first is that the wall mount routers are fucked and this might be the case for lots of people here (I even checked the insides and saw that RF shields were literally in pieces) and second that they are clipping the speed intentionally through a detection of sorts when there is an AP, I find that deranged tbh now legally I guess I cannot advise anything but I am going to just tell them to replace it, until then will buy a f-f ethernet adapter and connect the in/out directly and keep my mouth shut cause I remember someone already told here in this gc that using routers of your own were prohibited or sum, sorry about the wall of text tl; dr getting rid of a router altogether fixed connectivity and speed for me

[3/20/26, 9:45:19 AM] +49 1573 3660074: The Iranian new year, called Nowruz, begins at the exact moment of the spring equinox—when the sun crosses the earth’s equator and spring starts in the northern hemisphere. Because of this, the new year is based on astronomy and nature, not midnight on a calendar.
This year the new year moment is Friday, March 20 at 15:45:59 (3:45:59 PM) Germany time. At that exact second, we say “Happy Nowruz” and welcome the new year.
Nowruz is more than 3000 years old and celebrates spring, renewal, and hope.
Wishing you a beautiful spring 🌱

Real content. Your vocab. Effective fun.

In our early stage with the  MVP finally live, and we’re starting with German language.

[6/15/26, 11:35:58 PM] +49 176 82455992: Hey everyone 👋🏽:
I am Ibra. I’ve been building something for German learners called Xlang, an interactive media app for language learning and retention practice. 

I’m now starting a first community cohort.
Most of Xlang users like  like myself  (especially B1–B2) hit the same wall:
They understand some German…
but speaking still feels stuck.
So I’m starting a small, speaking-focused community to help fix that.
We’ll do:
• Weekly virtual speaking meetups
• Topic-based real-life conversations
• Accountability & consistency
• Using vocabulary from Xlang in actual speaking

It’s mainly for intermediate learners (B1–B2) who feel stuck in the plateau phase and want to start putting in consistent effort in speaking.
:rocket: First sessions kick off: June 27th & 28th

Access is currently limited to active Xlang users only.
If you want to join the first cohort, here’s the link:
https://chat.whatsapp.com/C2GD1s9ZaNIENxqhgFkQEv?s=cl&p=i&ilr=4
Would love to have you in the first group 🙂

I am trying to start an initiative after driving myself insane with attempting to find affordable German courses. My research concluded that self studying is the only way to not lose all my money on learning the language. 

So, I am offering to create a coworking space for my fellow impoverished people to meet and learn German together. I personally am significantly more productive when other people are around me, and it is hard to motivate myself when it is me and my four walls. Hence my offer to agree on a spot to meet up together and just each do our own thing or practice together (flashcards, reading texts, grammar exercises, speaking if you find somehow who wants to speak to you, etc.), motivating each other by our presence.

##### Moderne 2-Zimmer-Wohnung mit Gartenterrasse in Berlin– Nachmieter gesucht ##### ENGLISCH BELOW 

Zur Vermietung steht eine moderne und stilvolle Wohnung in einer exklusiven Wohnanlage in attraktiver Lage. Die U-Bahnstation Osloer Straße mit den Linien U8 und U9 ist nur wenige Minuten entfernt.

Die Wohnung befindet sich im Erdgeschoss und verfügt über eine großzügige Gartenterrasse mit Blick auf eine offene Gartenfläche. Rund um das Gebäude befinden sich gepflegte Grünanlagen, die für eine angenehme und ruhige Wohnatmosphäre sorgen.

Da unsere Familie wächst und wir deshalb in eine größere Wohnung umziehen, suchen wir einen passenden Nachmieter oder eine passende Nachmieterin.

### Wohnungsdetails ###
* ca. 55 m² Wohnfläche
* 2 Zimmer
* Badezimmer
* Fußbodenheizung
* großzügige Gartenterrasse
* großer und sauberer Kellerraum

### Verkehrsanbindung
* U-Bahnlinien U8 und U9 – Station Osloer Straße in unmittelbarer Nähe
* Straßenbahnlinien M13 und 50 schnell erreichbar
* Buslinien 125, 150 und 250 in der Nähe

### Aktuelle Mietkonditionen
Die folgenden Beträge entsprechen den aktuellen Zahlungen an die Hausverwaltung:
* Warmmiete: 1.250 € monatlich
* Kaltmiete: 970 € monatlich
* Kaution: 2.500 €
* unbefristeter Mietvertrag

### Ablöse: 3.000 €
Die Ablöse umfasst folgende Einrichtungsgegenstände:
* hochwertige Einbauküche inklusive Siemens-Geschirrspüler und Dunstabzugshaube
* moderne Glasabtrennung für die Badewanne
* Badezimmerspiegel
* Hochschrank für das Badezimmer
* hochwertige Terrassenschaukel
* weiße AEG-Waschmaschine

Bei Interesse kannst du uns gerne eine Nachricht schreiben. Anschließend vereinbaren wir einen Besichtigungstermin.

Nach der Besichtigung werden deine Unterlagen an die Hausverwaltung weitergeleitet. Die Hausverwaltung entscheidet, wer die Wohnung übernehmen darf.

########## 

##### Modern Two-Room Apartment with Garden Terrace in Berlin– New Tenant Wanted #####
A modern and stylish apartment is available in an exclusive residential complex in an attractive location. Osloer Straße underground station, served by the U8 and U9 lines, is only a few minutes away.
The apartment is located on the ground floor and features a spacious garden terrace overlooking an open garden area. The building is surrounded by well-maintained green spaces, creating a pleasant and peaceful living environment.
As our family is growing and we are moving to a larger apartment, we are looking for a suitable new tenant to take over the apartment.

### Apartment Details ###
* approximately 55 m² of living space
* 2 rooms
* bathroom
* underfloor heating
* garden terrace
* large and clean basement storage room

### Public Transport Connections ###
* U8 and U9 underground lines at Osloer Straße station within walking distance
* M13 and 50 tram lines easily accessible
* bus lines 125, 150 and 250 nearby

### Current Rental Conditions ###
The following amounts correspond to the payments currently made to the property management company:
* Monthly rent including utilities: €1,250
* Monthly base rent excluding utilities: €970
* Security deposit: €2,500
* Open-ended rental agreement

### Furniture and Equipment Transfer Fee: €3,000
The transfer fee includes the following items:
* high-quality fitted kitchen, including a Siemens dishwasher and extractor hood
* modern glass bathtub screen
* bathroom mirror
* tall bathroom cabinet
* high-quality terrace swing
* white AEG washing machine

Interested applicants are welcome to send us a message so that we can arrange a viewing appointment.
After the viewing, the applicants’ documents will be forwarded to the property management company. The final decision regarding who may take over the apartment will be made exclusively by the property management company.
